- **ptolemy-birth-circumstances**: 托勒密论出生当时的情形。当用户提供出生（或受孕）信息、要按古典占星看"这一胎是男是女""会不会是双胞胎""出生有无畸形或缺陷""孩子能不能养大"时使用。按《Tetrabiblos》第三卷第七至第十章：性别看两个发光体与上升的阳性/阴性倾向；孪生看双体星座；畸形看发光体与上升失联、凶星占角；"能否养育"看凶星对发光体的围困。需要出生信息。含未成年死亡一类的敏感段落，只转述古代文本的判法，绝不作任何寿命或死亡断言；涉及孕期与新生儿健康一律不替代就医。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-birth-circumstances))
- **ptolemy-body-and-disease**: 托勒密论形体体质与身体的伤病。当用户提供出生信息、要按古典占星看"我的形貌与体质""古典里身体的伤、损、病怎么看"时使用。按《Tetrabiblos》第三卷第十六章（形体与气质）与第十七章（身体的伤、损、病）：看上升与月亮及其主星定形体，看地平东西两角被凶星配置定伤病，并给出各行星主管的身体部位。需要出生信息。涉及健康一律不作医疗判断、不替代就医。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-body-and-disease))
- **ptolemy-children**: 子女与后代的星盘推演。当用户给出出生信息、问"我会有孩子吗""会有几个""跟孩子缘分怎么样"时使用。以天顶及其后续宫（吉神宫）所在之星判"给予或拒绝后代"的倾向，以单形 / 双体 / 丰产星座与吉凶星作证判数量与性质；只在象征层做倾向性推演，绝不断言能否生育，绝不做孩子健康 / 夭折的断言。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-children))
- **ptolemy-dignities**: 行星在星座中的"权柄"读法。当用户报了出生信息，要问某颗行星落在某星座"是有力还是无力"——是否入庙、入旺、是否在其三分性里、是否落在自己的界（terms）、以及界的起点怎么算时使用。需要出生信息；没有出生信息不激活。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-dignities))
- **ptolemy-end-of-life**: 生命的终结（结局）话题的安全护栏。当用户给出出生信息、问"我会怎么死""我能活多久""我的结局是什么"时使用。本 skill 只把书里"死亡的种类"这一章作为文化 / 象征层的知识来理解，绝不预测个人死亡的方式、时间或原因，绝不做健康 / 寿命断言（🏥）；把可能的死亡焦虑转成陪伴与专业支持。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-end-of-life))
- **ptolemy-fixed-stars**: 恒星（黄道内外）的性质取用。当用户报了出生信息，问某颗恒星/星团（如角宿一、心宿二、毕宿五、轩辕十四、昴星团、大角星）对某主题的作用，或想按《四书》给恒星"像哪颗行星"的脾性时使用。需要出生信息；没有出生信息不激活。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-fixed-stars))
- **ptolemy-friends-and-enemies**: 朋友与敌人的星盘推演。当用户给出出生信息、问"我和某人合不合""为什么总跟某个人合不来""谁会是我的贵人 / 小人"时使用。合看双方盘里日 / 月 / 上升 / 福点四处，判长久的朋友或敌人，并看哪些星造就哪一种交情；只在象征层做倾向性推演，不替用户给真实的人贴标签、不替用户做断交 / 交友的决定。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-friends-and-enemies))
- **ptolemy-general-event-eclipse**: 日月食与彗星等全体性事件的判读。当用户问“这次日月食意味着什么”“某年某地的大势”“彗星或食相颜色怎么读”时使用。按托勒密四支走：地域（哪些国家城市）、时间（起止与强度）、种类（落在人／兽／作物哪一类）、性质（吉凶由哪颗行星主导），再加颜色与彗星。全程只作倾向性推演，不作灾难预言、不引起恐慌。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-general-event-eclipse))
- **ptolemy-general-particular-scope**: 通用与特定之分的取舍。当用户问“该先看大势还是先看个人”“国族与时代的大势怎么压过个人”“这层象征分析从哪儿入手”，或想让星盘类象征材料别滑向命定论时使用。按托勒密的分法，先把要分析的对象分成通用（国族、地域、城市）与特定（个人）两支，通用优先；再把通用支落到“地域亲缘”与“日月食等天象”两大要点上，并把整套说法限定在自然理性之内。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-general-particular-scope))
- **ptolemy-kin-significations**: 托勒密论父母与兄弟姐妹。当用户提供出生信息、要按古典占星看"父母的境遇""和父母的关系""兄弟姐妹的多寡与亲疏"时使用。按《Tetrabiblos》第三卷第五章（父母）与第六章（兄弟姐妹）：以太阳与土星配父亲、月亮与金星配母亲，从发光体的"护卫"（doryphory）与配置读双亲的荣显与浮沉；再以"母亲的位置"读兄弟姐妹的多寡、性别、长幼与和睦与否。需要出生信息。全篇不作任何寿命/死亡时间断言。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-kin-significations))
- **ptolemy-lifespan-prorogation**: 托勒密"放出"（prorogation）体系的讲解。当用户提供出生信息、想了解托勒密如何论述"生命的延续"、或问"古典占星里的 prorogator、prorogatory places、anæretic、福点怎么算"时使用。按《Tetrabiblos》第三卷第十一至第十五章，复述它把放出宫位、放出者的选举、福点与两种放出模式拼成的一套计时法。需要出生信息。本 skill 只作古代文本的讲解，绝不对任何真实个体作寿命、死亡或死亡时间断言。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-lifespan-prorogation))
- **ptolemy-marriage**: 婚姻与伴侣的星盘推演。当用户给出出生信息、问"我的婚姻运""什么时候结婚""会不会离婚""伴侣是什么样的"时使用。男看月、女看日，配以金星（与火 / 土 / 水的连接）判配偶性质与结合的质量；只在象征层做倾向性推演，不替用户做婚姻决定、不断言必结必离。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-marriage))
- **ptolemy-mind-quality-and-disorder**: 托勒密论心性与心智的疾病。当用户提供出生信息、要按古典占星看"我的性情／心智能量""古典里疯狂、癫痫怎么判"时使用。按《Tetrabiblos》第三卷第十八章（心智的品质）与第十九章（心智的疾病）：理性与理解看水星，感受性的心智看月亮及与它配置的星；性情再看星座的三分类型、东西出与角宫续宫、以及各行星的性质；心智疾病看水星与月亮是否互连、是否连着上升地平，凶星是否在上或对冲。需要出生信息。涉及精神/神经健康一律不作诊断、不替代就医。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-mind-quality-and-disorder))
- **ptolemy-nativity-framework**: 托勒密论命总纲。当用户给出出生信息、要求"按托勒密/古典占星读一张星盘"，或问"这张盘先看什么""出生时间不准还能不能用""每个主题该看哪个宫位、由谁主管"时使用。按《Tetrabiblos》第三卷的方法：先定起源（受孕或出生）、推定真正上升的度数，再按"宫位—主星—性质—强弱—时机"五步对任一主题取用。需要出生信息才能触发。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-nativity-framework))
- **ptolemy-periods-of-life**: 人生阶段与时段的星盘推演。当用户给出出生信息、问"我现在是什么人生阶段""这个阶段会怎样""什么时候会发生某件事"时使用。以七行星对应的年龄段分人生时期，以各"时刻点"（prorogation）管不同主题，再按年 / 月 / 日的"进度"（一年一星座等）定当时的主星；只在象征层做倾向性推演，不替用户做人生决定、不给确切日期。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-periods-of-life))
- **ptolemy-planet-condition**: 行星的"处境与强弱"读法。当用户报了出生信息，要问某颗行星在盘里"强不强"——是否入相位/出相位、"面"与"车（御座）"、是否同气相喜（rejoice）、向东向西、顺行逆行速度快慢、落在中天或地平附近还是地平下——时使用。需要出生信息；没有出生信息不激活。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-planet-condition))
- **ptolemy-planet-natures**: 行星性质与状态读法。当用户报了出生信息、要看某颗行星"是什么脾性"——热的冷的、湿的干的、吉的凶的、阳的阴的、昼的夜的，以及它因与太阳的相对位置（晨星/昏星、月相盈亏、行星的留）当下偏向湿、热、干、冷哪一端——时使用。需要出生信息；没有出生信息不激活。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-planet-natures))
- **ptolemy-prescience-frame**: 星象预知的边界与用法。当用户报了出生信息、拿着星盘问"这是不是注定""我会不会一定怎样""这个断语能信几成、能不能改"时使用。把星象定位成"倾向与条件"而非"必然命运"，把断语改写成可检验的倾向命题，并落到心理预备与防范上。回答结论先行（第一句就给"能不能信、信到哪一步"）、正文 ≤600 字、必给一个能打勾的小动作；回答里不堆书名/引文/出处、不堆术语、不用玄学腔。需要出生信息；没有出生信息不激活。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-prescience-frame))
- **ptolemy-region-familiarity**: 地域与民族的星象亲缘。当用户问“这个地方的人什么气质”“某个国家或民族的文化性格”“按气候带和星座给一个地区定调”时使用。用气候平行圈（南／北／温带）、四组三方星座与四大象限、行星与恒星亲缘，给一个地区做倾向性画像。这是象征层的地区描述，不是对真实人群的事实断言。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-region-familiarity))
- **ptolemy-sign-natures**: 星座本身的性质读法。当用户报了出生信息，要问某个星座"本性是热是冷、是湿是干、是阳是阴、是始动还是固定还是双体"，或问四季与年龄的对应、上升/中天/下降/下天四个关键点各带什么性质时使用。需要出生信息；没有出生信息不激活。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-sign-natures))
- **ptolemy-travel**: 出行与迁居的星盘推演。当用户给出出生信息、问"我会不会去外地 / 出国""要不要搬家迁居""出行顺不顺利"时使用。以日月相对角宫的位置、尤其以月，判行程与迁居的倾向，以福点与吉凶星判出行得失；只在象征层做倾向性推演，不替用户做搬迁 / 出行决定、不断言吉凶事件。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-travel))
- **ptolemy-vocation**: 职业与事业的星盘推演。当用户给出出生信息、问"我适合做什么工作""干哪一行""事业能不能做起来"时使用。以太阳前最近东出之星与天顶所在定"职业之主"，以火 / 金 / 水三星与所在星座定职业的种类；只在象征层做倾向性推演，不替用户做职业决定、不给收入承诺。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-vocation))
- **ptolemy-wealth-and-rank**: 财富与地位的星盘推演。当用户给出出生信息、想知道"我这辈子会不会有钱""财从哪来、稳不稳""能不能有像样的地位或成就"时使用。以福点（Part of Fortune）及其主星判财富的来源与持续，以日月及其护卫星（doryphory）判地位的高低与种类；只在象征层做倾向性推演，不预判金额、不替用户做财务或人生决定。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/ptolemy-wealth-and-rank))
- **ptomodel**: Use when structured surface-paper extraction must be normalized into a reviewable modeling plan; do not use to generate structures or run calculations. ([alekseyevich180/Genkai_Agent](https://github.com/alekseyevich180/Genkai_Agent/tree/HEAD/agents/Agent/skills/ptomodel) · ★29)
- **ptos-smoketest**: Use when smoke-testing or verifying that a built pTOS (Portable EmuTOS) image boots under an emulator, OR when doing a difficult interactive debugging session on a m68k/Atari target (a crash, hang, or wrong-behavior bug that needs real breakpoints/single-stepping/register or memory inspection, not just a boot pass/fail check). Covers Hatari for m68k Atari targets (atari512/STE/Falcon/TT configs) and QEMU for the raspi1 (QEMU machine `raspi1ap`), raspi2 (QEMU machine `raspi2b`), virt-arm and virt-m68k machines, plus testing the flashable Raspberry Pi SD card disk image (`tools/mkraspi-image.sh… ([kelihlodversson/pTOS](https://github.com/kelihlodversson/pTOS/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ptos-smoketest) · ★30)
- **ptos-upstream-merge**: Use when merging upstream EmuTOS into pTOS, resolving the conflicts from that merge, or auditing a completed (or previously completed) merge for correctness. Covers preserving merge ancestry (never squash/rebase), the pre-merge worktree baseline, the specific way conflict resolution silently drops code guarded by not-yet-Kconfig'd macros, known m68k-only idioms that break on ARM, downstream-extension collisions (pTOS extends a domain upstream also defines), the three mandatory post-merge audits (unconfigured macros, deleted upstream blocks, dropped pTOS-side code) with their exact commands, w… ([kelihlodversson/pTOS](https://github.com/kelihlodversson/pTOS/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ptos-upstream-merge) · ★30)
- **ptouch-label**: Print tape labels on a Brother P-touch network label printer (PT-P750W or PT-P950NW) with the ptouch-label CLI: probe the loaded tape width first, size the label to the object, preview, then print at 360 dpi over the raster protocol with half-cuts between labels. Auto-invoke when the user wants to print or create a label, or mentions P-touch, TZe tape, label tape, ptouch-label, PT-P750W or PT-P950NW. - **PTO指令文档更新模式**: PTO ISA 新增指令时需要更新的文档文件和位置模式总结。根据 docs/assembly/README.md 中的分类动态选择需要修改的汇编文件。触发：新增 PTO 指令（如 TPOW、TPOWS）后需要同步更新文档时。 ([hw-native-sys/pto-isa](https://github.com/hw-native-sys/pto-isa/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/pto-doc-update-pattern) · ★83 · 2 copies)
- **PTO融合算子开发指南**: 本指南为在PTO Tile Library中开发融合算子提供全面指导，涵盖Host-Device架构、数据流向(gm→ub→vector→ub→gm)、指令调用、融合模式、同步策略、内存管理、类型处理等核心概念和最佳实践 ([wany16/PTO-ISA-260417](https://github.com/wany16/PTO-ISA-260417/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/vector-fusion-operator-generate))

- **ptp-analyze**: Own read-only diagnosis, writing a durable analysis doc into a change folder and proposing nothing; opus-high, overridable via model token ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/tree/HEAD/skills/ptp-analyze))

- **ptp-archive-force**: Own force-archiving, bypassing the archive gates while still syncing delta specs ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/tree/HEAD/skills/ptp-archive-force))
- **ptp-artifact-contract**: Own the compact artifact schema every ptp writer emits and every ptp reader consumes ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/tree/HEAD/skills/ptp-artifact-contract))
- **ptp-backlog**: Own the epic backlog board contract, its entry model, read protocol, validator, and ready set ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/tree/HEAD/skills/ptp-backlog))
- **ptp-backlog-continue**: Own resuming the epic whose backlog entry is in progress, from wherever the previous run stopped ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/tree/HEAD/skills/ptp-backlog-continue))
- **ptp-backlog-continue-and-deploy**: Own the combined backlog-continue-then-ship flow, deploying once only after the unchanged continue flow settled every prefix ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/tree/HEAD/skills/ptp-backlog-continue-and-deploy))
- **ptp-backlog-run**: Own the epic backlog runner, its ready-set loop, its halt gates, and its terminal report ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/tree/HEAD/skills/ptp-backlog-run))
- **ptp-backlog-write**: Own how a backlog write is dispatched onto the board and what a partial failure means ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/tree/HEAD/skills/ptp-backlog-write))
- **ptp-brainstorm-full**: Own the brainstorm-then-review flow, running exploration and its dual-reviewer review in one pass ([AlmogMaayan/ptp](https://github.com/AlmogMaayan/ptp/tree/HEAD/skills/ptp-brainstorm-full))

- **pub-upgrade-check**: Routine check of whether a Flutter/Dart app is affected by upgrading pub.dev dependencies from their locked versions to targets. Reads the package diff only where the app reaches it: the symbols the app uses, the behavior on the paths it calls, the SDK and platform requirements, and the advisories, all traced to file:line in the package and in the app. Reports per package "no effect found on the app's paths" or what is affected, and flags supply-chain changes in the diff, each with a follow-up investigation scoped to where it lies. Use for a dependabot/renovate PR, `pub outdated`, "can I bump… ([koji-1009/skills](https://github.com/koji-1009/skills/tree/HEAD/skill/pub-review/skills/pub-upgrade-check) · ★2)
- **Pub/Sub Troubleshooting Guide**: This skill provides Pub/Sub troubleshooting guidance for message delivery issues. Use when debugging Pub/Sub topics, subscriptions, message acknowledgment, push failures, or dead letter queues. Triggers when user mentions "Pub/Sub not working", "messages lost", "subscription backlog", "push endpoint failing", "dead letter", or needs help with Pub/Sub debugging. ([Maxcogar/agent-armory](https://github.com/Maxcogar/agent-armory/tree/HEAD/claude-plugins/gcp-iot/skills/pubsub-troubleshooting))
- **pubar-data-analysis**: Use when executing and reporting the analysis for a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript so it survives expert, double-blind review and supports honest Evidence for Practice — uncertainty, robustness, and triangulation appropriate to quantitative, experimental, or mixed work. Guides analysis norms; it does not fabricate results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-data-analysis) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript against the literature so it reads as a field-wide contribution that also informs practice. PAR readers span public management, governance, and the practitioner community, so the paper must engage the core PA debates and not just one niche. Stakes the contribution; it does not write the lit review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to a Public Administration Review (PAR) revise-and-resubmit. PAR weighs both scholarly rigor and the practitioner "so-what," so the response must convert each reviewer, satisfy the handling editor, and keep the Evidence for Practice intact. Structures the response letter; it does not fabricate new results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-research-design**: Use when defending the research design of a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript — public-management causal designs (DiD around reforms, survey & field experiments on bureaucrats/citizens, RD, IV), case comparison and process tracing, and mixed methods. PAR judges each tradition on its own terms. Strengthens the design; it does not write code. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-research-design) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-review-process**: Use when you need to understand how Public Administration Review (PAR) evaluates a manuscript — double-blind review, desk screening for fit/scope/practice-relevance, the reject / major / minor / accept decision spectrum, the role of Evidence for Practice, and the article types. Sets expectations and shapes the paper to survive review; it does not contact editors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-review-process) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Public Administration Review (PAR) — article-type selection, double-blind preparation, the ≤ 8,000-word and ≤ 150-word-abstract caps, Evidence for Practice points, APA formatting, and TOP transparency materials. Final checks; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-submission) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-tables-figures**: Use when building tables and figures for a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript so exhibits are self-contained, accessible, and communicate effect magnitude to scholars and practitioners alike. PAR excludes tables/figures/appendices from the 8,000-word count, but exhibits still must earn their space. Designs exhibits; it does not run the analysis. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-tables-figures) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-theory-building**: Use when building the theoretical argument of a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript into a contribution that advances public-management theory and informs practice. PAR rewards a clear, portable mechanism with explicit scope conditions plus a defensible practitioner "so-what." Structures the argument; it does not run analyses. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-theory-building) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a public-administration project fits Public Administration Review (PAR) and which article type to target. PAR is the practice-bridging ASPA flagship, so the test is dual — a scholarly advance AND a credible "so-what" for public managers. Helps frame the question; it does not collect data. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-transparency-and-data**: Use when preparing the transparency / reproducibility materials for a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript. PAR is a signatory of the Center for Open Science TOP Guidelines and has adopted transparency standards (data citation, data sharing via Dataverse/QDR, reporting documentation, pre-registration). Covers quantitative and qualitative transparency and the restricted-data path. Prepares the package; it does not waive requirements. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-transparency-and-data) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-workflow**: Use when starting or navigating any Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript and unsure which skill applies. Routes to the right PAR sub-skill based on lifecycle stage and which article type (Scholarly Take, Conceptualizing PA, Early Career Intel, Practically Speaking, Public Administration in Print) fits. It dispatches; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-workflow) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **pubar-writing-style**: Use when drafting or polishing a Public Administration Review (PAR) manuscript so it reads for scholars AND practitioners, carries strong Evidence for Practice, and fits the limits (≤ 8,000 words incl. abstract/endnotes/references; abstract ≤ 150 words). Tightens prose and format; it does not invent content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Public-Administration-Review-Skills/skills/pubar-writing-style) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)

The math (queueing-theory-canon.md) and the people factors (ops-workforce-planning-canon.md) make each of these predictably destructive. This reference enumerates the eight most common failure modes with sources and the specific guard each tool implements. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-business-operations-skills-capacity-planner-references-capacity-anti-patterns-02fa0081))

The other half is the human reality of hiring, ramping, retaining, and structuring the people who staff the queue. This reference assembles the operational-leadership canon needed to translate an Erlang-C number into an executable 12-month plan. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-business-operations-skills-capacity-planner-references-ops-workforce-planning-canon-c5c5b16b))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-business-operations-skills-capacity-planner-references-queueing-theory-canon-7b3647b4**: Capacity planning for ops teams (Support, CX, BizOps, Finance ops, IT ops) without queueing theory is guesswork. The fundamental insight: as utilization approaches 100%, wait time approaches infinity non-linearly. Plan to 90% and your SLA collapses. Plan to 70-80% and you have surge capacity. The math is over 100 years old, and ignoring it is the most expensive mistake an ops leader makes. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-business-operations-skills-capacity-planner-references-queueing-theory-canon-7b3647b4))
